 2962: function MapOn:FindNextMarker(direction)
 2963:   local startIndex = self.jumpToMarkerIndex
 2964:   local trackedMarker = false
 2965:   local cursorAtMarker = false
 2966:   repeat
 2967:     self.jumpToMarkerIndex = self.jumpToMarkerIndex + direction
 2968:     if self.jumpToMarkerIndex < 0 then
 2969:       self.jumpToMarkerIndex = #self.markers
 2970:     end
 2971:     if self.jumpToMarkerIndex > #self.markers then
 2972:       self.jumpToMarkerIndex = 0
 2973:     end
 2974:     trackedMarker = self.jumpToMarkerIndex == 0 or self:GetMapMarkerTrackingState(self.markers[self.jumpToMarkerIndex].Id) == questConsts.TRACKING_STATE_TRACKED
 2975:     cursorAtMarker = self.jumpToMarkerIndex == 0 and self.currMarkerPlayerIcon or self.jumpToMarkerIndex ~= 0 and self.currMarkerID ~= nil and self.markers[self.jumpToMarkerIndex].Id == self.currMarkerID
 2976:   until not (not trackedMarker or cursorAtMarker) or startIndex == self.jumpToMarkerIndex
 2977:   if self.jumpToMarkerIndex == 0 and not self:ShouldShowPlayerMarker() and mapUtil.GetPlayerRealm() == self.currRealmName and self.markers[1] ~= nil and self:GetMapMarkerTrackingState(self.markers[1].Id) == questConsts.TRACKING_STATE_TRACKED then
 2978:     self.jumpToMarkerIndex = 1
 2979:   end
 2980: end
 2981: function MapOn:JumpToMarker()
 2982:   if self.jumpToMarkerIndex == 0 and self:ShouldShowPlayerMarker() then
 2983:     Camera.PointAtGO(self.playerIconGO)
 2984:   elseif self.markers[self.jumpToMarkerIndex] ~= nil then
 2985:     Camera.PointAtGO(self.markers[self.jumpToMarkerIndex].iconGO)
 2986:   end
 2987: end

 4962:   function MapOn:ShowOnCompass(currState)
 4963:     lastMapOnSelf = self
 4964:     local current = currentSelection(self)
 4965:     if currentMarkerDiffersFromSelection(self, current) then
 4966:       clearSelection(self, "confirmed_native_action:marker=" .. tostring(self.currMarkerID))
 4967:     elseif current ~= nil and not customRavenOwnsAction(self, current) then
 4968:       clearSelection(self, "custom_prompt_owner_lost_before_action")
 4969:     elseif current ~= nil and current.State ~= "armed-custom" then
 4970:       clearSelection(self, "action_without_prompt_arm")
 4971:     end
 4972:     local selected = consumeSelection(self)
 4973:     if selected == nil then
 4974:       -- Stock actions stay native. Hide only active Twin first because the older
 4975:       -- controller knows real Raven but predates Twin UID.
 4976:       local twin = identity(twinName, "twin")
 4977:       if twin ~= nil then
 4978:         local twinShown, queryOK = customShown(twin)
 4979:         if queryOK and twinShown then
 4980:           local ok, err = pcall(function() game.Compass.HideMarker(twinName) end)
 4981:           if not ok then
 4982:             log("STOCK_REPLACE_TWIN_REFUSED", "uid=" .. twin.IdString ..
 4983:                 " reason=twin_hide_failed error=" .. tostring(err) ..
 4984:                 " nativeDelegated=false")

  888: local function CompletionistMapV100_Description()
  889:   local target = _G.CompletionistMapV100Target
  890:   if target ~= nil and target.type == "Raven" and target.active then
  891:     return "Remaining collectible - tracked on custom compass"
  892:   end
  893:   return "Remaining collectible - Completionist Map"
  894: end
  895: 
  896: local function CompletionistMapV100_ClearStockCompass(self, reason)
  897:   if self.currShownMarkerID == nil then
  898:     return
  899:   end
  900: 
  901:   local oldId = self.currShownMarkerID
  902:   local hideOK, hideErr = pcall(function()
  903:     game.Compass.HideMarker(oldId)
  904:   end)
  905: 
  906:   self.currShownMarkerID = nil
  907: 
  908:   print("[CompletionistMap v0.10.1] STOCK_COMPASS_CLEAR" ..
  909:     " reason=" .. tostring(reason) ..
  910:     " id=" .. tostring(oldId) ..
  911:     " ok=" .. tostring(hideOK) ..
  912:     " error=" .. tostring(hideErr))
  913: end

  899:   end

 2271: function MapOn:GetMapMarkerTrackingState(markerID)
 2272:   local trackingState = questConsts.TRACKING_STATE_NONE
 2273:   local hasQuest, questID = questUtil.FindQuestForMarker(markerID)
 2274:   if hasQuest then
 2275:     local trackingInfo = questUtil.GetTrackingInfo(questID)

 2277:   end

 2279:     trackingState = questConsts.TRACKING_STATE_TRACKED
 2280:   end
 2281:   return trackingState
